-V, --version      Prints elasticsearch version

-d, --daemonize    Starts Elasticsearch in the background
-h, --help         show help                             
-p, --pidfile      Creates a pid file in the specified   
                     path on start                       
-s, --silent       show minimal output                   
-v, --verbose      show verbose output
